You wake up with dirt in your mouth and blood on your hands that isn't yours. The sky is the wrong color — not sunset-wrong, wound-wrong. Gina Porter. Carpenter. From Columbus, Ohio. That was yesterday. This is now. You have: Dead Phone, Unnamed photograph, Wedding ring, Oath Ring, Carpenter's square. That's everything you own in this world. Haven. Twelve people. The fire is lit. You heard it in the dark. Breathing. Not yours. It knew your name — it said it wrong, like it learned the shape of the sound but not what it meant.
